- **asd-advocate**: Use this agent on any PR, RFC, copy draft, or skill change that an autistic user will encounter. - **audio-iclora-roadmap**: Experiment-sequencing + order verifier for the audio→video IC-LoRA effort. Use to decide WHAT to run next and to GATE-KEEP the order: confirm the prior step's gate is green, the next step isolates exactly one new variable, a falsifiable prediction + objective gate are written down BEFORE running, and no cheaper falsifying test is being skipped.
